Q1. 80 pages are printed per hour for 5 hours, but only 90% are accepted. Find the accepted pages.
A. 361 pages
B. 355 pages
C. 360 pages
D. 270 pages
Answer: 360 pages
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_work_quality_rejection
Explanation:
Use the governing rate-state formula.
\[
Accepted=Produced\times \frac{p}{100}
\]
Substitute the given values.
\[
Accepted=80\times 5\times \frac{90}{100}
\]
Simplify to get the required value.
\[
Accepted=360
\]
Therefore the required answer is obtained.
\[
Ans=360
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
Accepted=gross\times p/100
\]
Answer: 360 pages

---

Q2. Workers contribute in the ratio 8:12 to a ₹12000 job. Find the asked share.
A. ₹4795
B. ₹4798
C. ₹3600
D. ₹4800
Answer: ₹4800
Difficulty: Easy
Family: tw_wage_partial_time
Explanation:
Use the governing rate-state formula.
\[
Share=\frac{c_i}{\sum c}\times W
\]
Substitute the given values.
\[
Share=\frac{8}{20}\times 12000
\]
Simplify to get the required value.
\[
Share=4800
\]
Therefore the required answer is obtained.
\[
Ans=4800
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
Share=contribution\ ratio\times wage
\]
Answer: ₹4800

---

Q3. One team completes 12 units per day while another undoes 4 units per day. For 180 units, find the net completion time.
A. 27.5 days
B. 16.88 days
C. 19.5 days
D. 22.5 days
Answer: 22.5 days
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_negative_positive_mixed_work
Explanation:
Use the governing rate-state formula.
\[
T=\frac{W}{r_+-r_-}
\]
Substitute the given values.
\[
T=\frac{180}{12-4}
\]
Simplify to get the required value.
\[
T=22.5
\]
Therefore the required answer is obtained.
\[
Ans=22.5
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=W/net\ rate
\]
Answer: 22.5 days

---

Q5. A can finish a work in 12 days and B in 18 days. A works alone for 4 days, then B joins. Find the total time taken.
A. 7.8 days
B. 8.8 days
C. 11 days
D. 3.8 days
Answer: 8.8 days
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_delayed_join
Explanation:
Use the governing rate-state formula.
\[
W_1=r_At_1,\ T_2=\frac{W-W_1}{r_A+r_B}
\]
Substitute the given values.
\[
W_1=15\times 4,\quad R=180-60
\]
Simplify to get the required value.
\[
T=4+\frac{120}{15+10}=8.8
\]
Therefore the required answer is obtained.
\[
Ans=8.8
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=4+\frac{180-15\times 4}{15+10}
\]
Answer: 8.8 days

---

T=W/R

Q9. The full team takes 8 days. Removing known rates with individual times 12 days, find the remaining worker's time.
A. 20 days
B. 28.8 days
C. 24 days
D. 23 days
Answer: 24 days
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_pairwise_total_abc
Explanation:
Use the governing rate-state formula.
\[
r_X=r_{team}-\sum r_i
\]
Substitute the given values.
\[
r_X=\frac{1}{8}-\frac{1}{12}
\]
Simplify to get the required value.
\[
T_X=24
\]
Therefore the required answer is obtained.
\[
Ans=24
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T_X=1/r_X
\]
Answer: 24 days

Q10. A worker does 15 units per day for 5 days, then slows to 12 units per day. Total work is 180 units. Find total time.
A. 8.75 days
B. 13.75 days
C. 17.19 days
D. 10.75 days
Answer: 13.75 days
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_productivity_decay
Explanation:
Use the governing rate-state formula.
\[
T=t_1+\frac{W-r_1t_1}{r_2}
\]
Substitute the given values.
\[
T=5+\frac{180-15\times 5}{12}
\]
Simplify to get the required value.
\[
T=13.75
\]
Therefore the required answer is obtained.
\[
Ans=13.75
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=phase1+phase2
\]
Answer: 13.75 days

Q11. A job changes team after 4 days. A, B and C alone take 12, 18 and 24 days. If B and C join, find the completion time.
A. 7.69 days
B. 11.54 days
C. 4.69 days
D. 11.69 days
Answer: 7.69 days
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_multi_phase_join_leave
Explanation:
Use the governing rate-state formula.
\[
\sum r_it_i=W
\]
Substitute the given values.
\[
W_1=6\times 4
\]
Simplify to get the required value.
\[
T=4+\frac{48}{13}=7.69
\]
Therefore the required answer is obtained.
\[
Ans=7.69
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=t_1+\frac{W-W_1}{r_2}
\]
Answer: 7.69 days

Q14. Normally a tank fills in 15 hours, but with leakage it takes 25 hours. Find the leak's emptying time.
A. 42.5 hours
B. 37.5 hours
C. 28.13 hours
D. 39.5 hours
Answer: 37.5 hours
Difficulty: Medium
Family: pc_leak_hidden_rate
Explanation:
Use the governing rate-state formula.
\[
\frac{1}{L}=\frac{1}{F}-\frac{1}{T}
\]
Substitute the given values.
\[
\frac{1}{L}=\frac{1}{15}-\frac{1}{25}
\]
Simplify to get the required value.
\[
L=37.5
\]
Therefore the required answer is obtained.
\[
Ans=37.5
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
L=\frac{FT}{T-F}
\]
Answer: 37.5 hours

---

Q15. Working alone, A takes 10 days and B takes 20 days for the same work. Find their combined time.
A. 15 days
B. 10 days
C. 6.67 days
D. 30 days
Answer: 6.67 days
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_lcm_hidden_total_work
Explanation:
Use the governing rate-state formula.
\[
W=LCM(10,20)=20
\]
Substitute the given values.
\[
A=20/10=2,\quad B=20/20=1
\]
Simplify to get the required value.
\[
T=\frac{20}{2+1}=6.67
\]
Therefore the required answer is obtained.
\[
Ans=6.67
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=\frac{10\times 20}{10+20}=6.67
\]
Answer: 6.67 days

---

Q17. Two workers have efficiencies 5:7. Their combined time is 8 days. Find A's individual time.
A. 20 days
B. 11.2 days
C. 19.2 days
D. 5.71 days
Answer: 19.2 days
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_relative_efficiency_percent
Explanation:
Use the governing rate-state formula.
\[
W=(a+b)T
\]
Substitute the given values.
\[
W=(5+7)\times 8
\]
Simplify to get the required value.
\[
T_A=\frac{96}{5}=19.2
\]
Therefore the required answer is obtained.
\[
Ans=19.2
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T_A=\frac{(a+b)T}{a}=\frac{(5+7)8}{5}=19.2
\]
Answer: 19.2 days

\[

\]

\[
D=\frac{720-24\times 6}{30}
\]
Simplify to get the required value.
\[
D=19.2
\]
Therefore the required answer is obtained.
\[
Ans=19.2
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
D=remaining\ stock/final\ people
\]
Answer: 19.2 days

Q22. If \(\frac{1}{3}\) of a job takes 12 days, how many days will the full job take?

Family: tw_work_fraction_days
Explanation:

\[

\[
T=12\times \frac{3}{1}
\]
Simplify to get the required value.
\[
T=36
\]
Therefore the required answer is obtained.
\[
Ans=36
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=12\div \frac{1}{3}=36
\]
Answer: 36 days

\[

Ans=1.67

Q28. A job has 180 units. 12 workers finish 72 units, and 8 days are left. Find the extra workers required.
A. 2 workers
B. 2.4 workers
C. 1 worker
D. 0.5 workers
Answer: 2 workers
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_contract_deadline_extra_workers
Explanation:
Use the governing rate-state formula.
\[
extra=\left\lceil\frac{R}{r\times t}\right\rceil-current
\]
Substitute the given values.
\[
extra=\left\lceil\frac{108}{1\times 8}\right\rceil-12
\]
Simplify to get the required value.
\[
extra=2
\]
Therefore the required answer is obtained.
\[
Ans=2
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
extra=required-current
\]
Answer: 2 workers

Q31. A and B begin a job together. After 5 days A leaves. If A and B alone take 18 and 15 days, find when the job ends.
A. 6.83 days
B. 16.25 days
C. 10.83 days
D. 13.83 days
Answer: 10.83 days
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_forward_leave
Explanation:
Use the governing rate-state formula.
\[
W=(r_A+r_B)t_1+r_Bt_2
\]
Substitute the given values.
\[
W_1=(35+42)\times 5
\]
Simplify to get the required value.
\[
T=5+\frac{245}{42}=10.83
\]
Therefore the required answer is obtained.
\[
Ans=10.83
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=5+\frac{W-W_1}{B}
\]
Answer: 10.83 days

Q35. Pair times are AB=10 days, BC=12 days and CA=15 days. Find the time taken by A alone.
A. 25 days
B. 24 days
C. 18 days
D. 19 days
Answer: 24 days
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_pairwise_ab_bc_ac
Explanation:
Use the governing rate-state formula.
\[
2(A+B+C)=(A+B)+(B+C)+(C+A)
\]
Substitute the given values.
\[
AB=10,\ BC=12,\ CA=15
\]
Simplify to get the required value.
\[
T_A=24
\]
Therefore the required answer is obtained.
\[
Ans=24
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
A=\frac{AB+AC-BC}{2}
\]
Answer: 24 days

---

Q36. A filling pipe adds 12 units per hour and an emptying pipe removes 5 units in the next hour alternately. Tank capacity is 60 units. Find the filling time.
A. 20.42 hours
B. 15.33 hours
C. 16.33 hours
D. 11.33 hours
Answer: 16.33 hours
Difficulty: Hard
Family: pc_alternating_fill_empty
Explanation:
Use the governing rate-state formula.
\[
W_{cycle}=12-5
\]

\[
Accepted=Produced\times \frac{p}{100}
\]
Substitute the given values.
\[
Accepted=100\times 8\times \frac{95}{100}
\]
Simplify to get the required value.
\[
Accepted=760
\]
Therefore the required answer is obtained.
\[
Ans=760
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
Accepted=gross\times p/100
\]
Answer: 760 pages

6(T-2)+15(T-3)=180
\]
Simplify to get the required value.
\[
T=11.29
\]
Therefore the required answer is obtained.
\[
Ans=11.29
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=\frac{W+A\times 2+B\times 3}{A+B}
\]
Answer: 11.29 days

Q492. A alone needs 12 days, while A and B together need 6 days. How many days will B alone need?
A. 9 days
B. 6 days
C. 18 days
D. 12 days
Answer: 12 days
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_individual_from_combined
Explanation:
Use the governing rate-state formula.
\[
\frac{1}{B}=\frac{1}{T}-\frac{1}{A}
\]
Substitute the given values.
\[
\frac{1}{B}=\frac{1}{6}-\frac{1}{12}
\]
Simplify to get the required value.
\[
B=12
\]
Therefore the required answer is obtained.
\[
Ans=12
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
B=\frac{6\times 12}{12-6}=12
\]
Answer: 12 days

\]
Simplify to get the required value.
\[
T=16.8
\]
Therefore the required answer is obtained.

Ans=16.8
\]

\]

Q494. A finishes in 20 days and B in 8 days. Find their efficiency ratio.

B. 2:5

W_A:W_B=8:20
\]

W_A:W_B=2:5
\]
Therefore the required answer is obtained.

Ans=2:5
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[

\]

Q495. A, B and C alone take 15, 20 and 18 days. For the first 4 days the initial phase runs, then B is replaced by C. Find the total time.
A. 12.36 days
B. 8.36 days
C. 10.36 days
D. 12.55 days
Answer: 8.36 days
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_replacement_worker
Explanation:
Use the governing rate-state formula.
\[
r_1t_1+r_2t_2=W
\]
Substitute the given values.
\[
W_1=21\times 4
\]
Simplify to get the required value.
\[
T=4+\frac{96}{22}=8.36
\]
Therefore the required answer is obtained.
\[
Ans=8.36
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=t_1+\frac{W-W_1}{r_2}
\]
Answer: 8.36 days

Q497. 900 person-days of food are available. 24 people consume it for 6 days, then the group becomes 29. Find how many more days the food will last.

B. 31.28 days
C. 26.07 days
D. 24.07 days

Family: tw_food_remaining_stock
Explanation:

\[
D=\frac{S-consumed}{people}
\]

\[
D=\frac{900-24\times 6}{29}
\]
Simplify to get the required value.
\[
D=26.07
\]
Therefore the required answer is obtained.
\[
Ans=26.07
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
D=remaining\ stock/final\ people
\]
Answer: 26.07 days

Q498. A work is planned to finish in 30 days. After 8 days of work, find the remaining fraction.
A. 11/15
B. 11/4
C. 4/15
D. 15/11
Answer: 11/15
Difficulty: Easy
Family: tw_basic_residual_work
Explanation:
Use the governing rate-state formula.
\[
R=1-\frac{t}{T}
\]
Substitute the given values.
\[
R=1-\frac{8}{30}
\]
Simplify to get the required value.
\[
R=\frac{22}{30}=11/15
\]
Therefore the required answer is obtained.
\[
Ans=11/15
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
R=\frac{30-8}{30}=11/15
\]
Answer: 11/15

Q499. Food for 20 people lasts 36 days. How many days will it last for 40 people?
A. 21 days
B. 22 days
C. 14.4 days
D. 18 days
Answer: 18 days
Difficulty: Easy
Family: tw_food_resource_basic
Explanation:
Use the governing rate-state formula.
\[
M_1D_1=M_2D_2
\]
Substitute the given values.
\[
20\times 36=40\times D_2
\]
Simplify to get the required value.
\[
D_2=18
\]
Therefore the required answer is obtained.
\[
Ans=18
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
D_2=\frac{20\times 36}{40}
\]
Answer: 18 days

Q500. If a job takes 18 days and 3 days have already been worked, what part is still unfinished?
A. 5/1
B. 5/6
C. 6/5
D. 1/6
Answer: 5/6
Difficulty: Easy
Family: tw_remaining_work_fraction
Explanation:
Use the governing rate-state formula.
\[
R=1-\frac{t}{T}
\]
Substitute the given values.
\[
R=1-\frac{3}{18}
\]
Simplify to get the required value.
\[
R=\frac{15}{18}=5/6
\]
Therefore the required answer is obtained.
\[
Ans=5/6
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
R=\frac{18-3}{18}=5/6
\]
Answer: 5/6
